percentile_inc(values, k):\n    \"\"\"Excel PERCENTILE (inclusive) behavior.\"\"\"\n    vals = sorted(values)\n    n = len(vals)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    if k <= 0:\n        return  return float(vals[lo - 1])\n    # interpolate\n    d = pos - lo\n    return float(vals[lo - 1] + d * (vals[hi - 1] - vals[lo - 1]))\n\n# Build value dict for all formula cells we touched\ncomputed = {}\n\n# Lookup blocks values\nfor (r0, r1) in LOOKUP_BLOCKS:\n    for r in range(r0, r1 + 1):\n        series = task.cell(r, 4).value\n        if series is None:\n            continue\n        series = str(series)\n        if series not in series_to_row:\n            raise RuntimeError(f'Series code {series} not found in Data!D21:D38')\n        dr = series_to_row[series]\n        for c in TASK_YEAR_COLS:\n            year = int(task.cell(TASK_YEAR_ROW, c).value)\n            dc = year_to_col[year]\n            val = data.cell(dr, dc).value\n            coord = f\"{get_column_letter(c)}{r}\"\n            computed[coord] = float(val) if val is not None else None\n\n# Net flow values\nfor i in range(6):\n    in_r = 12 + i\n    out_r = 19 + i\n    cap_r = 26 + i\n    flow_r = 35 + i\n    for c in TASK_YEAR_COLS:\n        colL = get_column_letter(c)\n        in_v = computed[f\"{colL}{in_r}\"]\n        out_v = computed[f\"{colL}{out_r}\"]\n        cap_v = computed[f\"{colL}{cap_r}\"]\n        flow = (in_v - out_v) / cap_v * 100\n        computed[f\"{colL}{flow_r}\"] = float(flow)\n\n# Statistics\nfor c in TASK_YEAR_COLS:\n    colL = get_column_letter(c)\n    flows = [computed[f\"{colL}{35+i}\"] for i in range(6)]\n    computed[f\"{colL}42\"] = float(min(flows))\n    computed[f\"{colL}43\"] = float(max(flows))\n    s = sorted(flows)\n    # median\n    if len(s) % 2 == 1:\n        med = s[len(s)//2]\n    else:\n        med = (s[len(s)//2 - 1] + s[len(s)//2]) / 2\n    computed[f\"{colL}44\"] = float(med)\n    computed[f\"{colL}45\"] = float(sum(flows) / len(flows))\n    computed[f\"{colL}46\"] = float(percentile_inc(flows, 0.25))\n    computed[f\"{colL}47\"] = float(percentile_inc(flows, 0.75))\n\n# Weighted mean\nfor c in TASK_YEAR_COLS:\n    colL = get_column_letter(c)\n    flows = [computed[f\"{colL}{35+i}\"] for i in range(6)]\n    caps = [computed[f\"{colL}{26+i}\"] for i in range(6)]\n    denom = sum(caps)\n    num = sum(f*w for f, w in zip(flows, caps))\n    computed[f\"{colL}50\"] = float(num / denom)\n\n# --- Inject cached values into Task sheet XML ---\n\nNS_MAIN = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/spreadsheetml/2006/main'\nNS_REL = 'http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/officeDocument/2006/relationships'\n\nET.register_namespace('', NS_MAIN)\nET.register_namespace('r', NS_REL)\n\ndef get_task_sheet_path(xlsx_path):\n    with zipfile.ZipFile(xlsx_path, 'r') as z:\n        wb_xml = z.read('xl/workbook.xml')\n        rels_xml = z.read('xl/_rels/workbook.xml.rels')\n\n    wb_root = ET.fromstring(wb_xml)\n    rels_root = ET.fromstring(rels_xml)\n\n    # Find r:id for sheet name=\"Task\"\n    rid = None\n    for sh in wb_root.findall(f\".//{{{NS_MAIN}}}sheet\"):\n        if sh.attrib.get('name') == 'Task':\n            rid = sh.attrib.get(f\"{{{NS_REL}}}id\")\n            break\n    if rid is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('Could not find Task sheet r:id in workbook.xml')\n\n    target = None\n    for rel in rels_root.findall(f\".//{{http://schemas.openxmlformats.org/package/2006/relationships}}Relationship\"):\n        if rel.attrib.get('Id') == rid:\n            target = rel.attrib.get('Target')\n            break\n    if target is None:\n        raise RuntimeError('Could not resolve Task sheet target in workbook.xml.rels')\n\n    # Target is like 'worksheets/sheet1.xml'\n    if target.startswith('/'):\n        target = target.lstrip('/')\n    if not target.startswith('xl/'):\n        target = 'xl/' + target\n    return target\n\nTASK_SHEET_XML =
